# Commands · G · part 8

1,390 entries, grill to gstack-design-consultation. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **grill**: Adversarial data-engineering review of the branch; no SHIP IT until every issue is fixed ([ryanosborne142980-cpu/medallion-synapse-lab](https://github.com/ryanosborne142980-cpu/medallion-synapse-lab/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/grill.md))
- **grill**: Interrogate an idea before it becomes a spec — one grounded question at a time, each with a recommended answer, until every branch is resolved. Writes a decision record (grill.md) that /spec builds from. No code, no spec, just decisions and the reasons behind them. ([sechang11/Agent0-Context-Framework](https://github.com/sechang11/Agent0-Context-Framework/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/grill.md))
- **grill**: Phỏng vấn dồn dập để làm rõ một ý tưởng/kế hoạch/quyết định trước khi code — hỏi theo đợt, mỗi câu kèm đề xuất, tự tra sự kiện, dừng khi hết mơ hồ; gợi ý /adr khi có quyết định khó đảo ([seeker19110/donghanh](https://github.com/seeker19110/donghanh/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/grill.md) · 2 copies)
- **grill**: Produce a plain-language, zero-jargon human briefing of what changed, what was verified, and what's still risky. Use after a stage or review. ([seokho1215-ux/nb-harness](https://github.com/seokho1215-ux/nb-harness/blob/HEAD/commands/grill.md))
- **grill**: `/aico:grill &lt;idea | file | resume &lt;slug&gt;&gt;` ([therealmcsparrow/McAICompany](https://github.com/therealmcsparrow/McAICompany/blob/HEAD/documentation/commands/grill.md))
- **grill**: Grill — adversarial code review that attacks implementation quality and forces you to defend your choices ([tomashermansen-lang/watchfloor](https://github.com/tomashermansen-lang/watchfloor/blob/HEAD/adapters/claude-code/claude/commands/grill.md))
- **grill**: Bare grill session — the interview front door with no preset. ([Truncuso/elicitforge](https://github.com/Truncuso/elicitforge/blob/HEAD/adapters/claude-code/commands/grill.md))
- **grill**: Standalone adversarial design-grill — the design-level mirror of `/review`. Runs by invocation between `/plan` and `/breakdown` to attack the FINISHED design (`plan.md` + its `spec.md`) before `/breakdown` spends effort decomposing it. Dispatches the `devils-advocate` adversary (which resolves a three-ring codebase blast radius and self-gated web-verification), cross-examines every attack with a non-author refutation pass (architect excluded), writes `specs/[feature]/grill.md`, and recommends a 4-way disposition (PROCEED / REVISE-PLAN / RE-ENTER-UPSTREAM / KILL). Opt-in — never an auto-gate. ([webmint/mintEnvoy](https://github.com/webmint/mintEnvoy/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/grill.md))
- **grill**: Adversarial pre-ship code review of the current branch ([wonrhee/claude-base-kit](https://github.com/wonrhee/claude-base-kit/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/grill.md))
- **grill**: 一次一个问题，把模糊的想法问成 docs/brief.md；已有计划时做变更访谈，写成简报里的“变更 N” ([XLBen/skill](https://github.com/XLBen/skill/blob/HEAD/.opencode/commands/grill.md))
- **grill**: Interview-style grilling of an existing plan, spec, or design against the project's CONTEXT.md glossary and recorded ADRs. Surfaces contradictions, sharpens fuzzy terms, captures resolutions inline. ([zsolt-audiotrader/audiotrader-skills](https://github.com/zsolt-audiotrader/audiotrader-skills/blob/HEAD/audiotrader-workflow/commands/grill.md))

- **grill-and-document**: Grill decisions while updating the glossary and ADR trail. ([IMisbahk/mstack](https://github.com/IMisbahk/mstack/blob/HEAD/distribution/mstack/commands/grill-and-document.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)

- **grill-design**: 呼叫 `auto-grill` workflow,用 1 提問 + 3 多視角評審 + 裁決者 + 記錄者, 把任何設計樹收斂到具體決定 + 留下持久記錄。技術選型必查最新資料附 URL 來源。 ([Leon180/workingbad](https://github.com/Leon180/workingbad/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/grill-design.md) · ★1)

- **groundwork**: Set up or route work through the project's context system and lanes ([Exilitys/ExilitysSkills](https://github.com/Exilitys/ExilitysSkills/blob/HEAD/plugins/groundwork/commands/groundwork.md))
- **groundwork**: Start Groundwork agent to turn a spec — extracted (rebuild) or a human-authored functional spec (greenfield) — into a drainable board: confirmed stack, .docs/ + AI.md/CLAUDE.md scaffold, walking-skeleton + chassis initiation backlog (one-shot). With no spec (or --init), Init mode scaffolds the adoption artifacts only. ([gthio/random-20260909-the-dust-ui](https://github.com/gthio/random-20260909-the-dust-ui/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/groundwork.md))
- **groundwork-adr**: Author an Architecture Decision Record. Captures the why behind a choice so future agents and humans do not have to re-derive it. Triggers on "write an ADR for...", "document why we chose...", "we just decided X, capture it", "create a decision record", "/gw adr". ([IgniticAB/groundwork](https://github.com/IgniticAB/groundwork/blob/HEAD/skills/groundwork/commands/adr.md) · ★1)
- **groundwork-apply**: Read a remediation plan produced by the `audit` command's interactive HTML report and apply the chosen fixes to the repo. Closes the loop between audit and action. Triggers on phrases like "apply this remediation", "run the audit fixes", "`/gw apply &lt;file&gt;`". ([IgniticAB/groundwork](https://github.com/IgniticAB/groundwork/blob/HEAD/skills/groundwork/commands/apply.md) · ★1)
- **groundwork-audit**: Score the repo's context engineering maturity across the five layers and produce an action plan. Use when the user asks "is my repo AI-ready?", "how good is our context setup?", "audit our CLAUDE.md", or before a major migration / handoff / new-hire onboarding. Also triggers for "/gw audit" and "context audit". ([IgniticAB/groundwork](https://github.com/IgniticAB/groundwork/blob/HEAD/skills/groundwork/commands/audit.md) · ★1)
- **groundwork-capture**: Turn subagent transcript capture on or off for this project. ([amitbaz/ai-marketplace](https://github.com/amitbaz/ai-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/groundwork/commands/groundwork-capture.md))
- **groundwork-document**: Scan the codebase and produce or refresh CLAUDE.md / AGENTS.md / .cursor/rules/ with current, correct information. Use this when the repo already has some context files but they are stale, or when adopting context engineering on a mature repo. Triggers on "refresh CLAUDE.md", "update our context files", "document this codebase for the agent", "/gw document". ([IgniticAB/groundwork](https://github.com/IgniticAB/groundwork/blob/HEAD/skills/groundwork/commands/document.md) · ★1)
- **groundwork-drift**: Audit whether the project's context files are still true ([Exilitys/ExilitysSkills](https://github.com/Exilitys/ExilitysSkills/blob/HEAD/plugins/groundwork/commands/groundwork-drift.md))
- **groundwork-hooks**: Install the contract gate and session-start hooks into this repo ([Exilitys/ExilitysSkills](https://github.com/Exilitys/ExilitysSkills/blob/HEAD/plugins/groundwork/commands/groundwork-hooks.md))
- **groundwork-hunt**: Hunt a groundwork record for what is missing from it ([ananmouaz/groundwork](https://github.com/ananmouaz/groundwork/blob/HEAD/commands/groundwork-hunt.md) · ★1)
- **groundwork-init**: Bootstrap a repo with the full context engineering scaffolding. Run this on a new repo or an existing repo that has no AGENTS.md/CLAUDE.md/.cursor/rules/ yet. Triggers on phrases like "initialize this repo for AI", "set up context engineering", "bootstrap AGENTS.md", "make this AI-first", "/gw init". ([IgniticAB/groundwork](https://github.com/IgniticAB/groundwork/blob/HEAD/skills/groundwork/commands/init.md) · ★1)
- **groundwork-mcp**: Design a Model Context Protocol (MCP) configuration with least-privilege, human-in-the-loop, and auditing baked in. Use this before connecting an agent to a real system (database, deploy pipeline, API, file store). Triggers on "set up MCP for...", "give the agent access to...", "design an MCP server", "least privilege agent access", "/gw mcp". ([IgniticAB/groundwork](https://github.com/IgniticAB/groundwork/blob/HEAD/skills/groundwork/commands/mcp.md) · ★1)
- **groundwork-onboard**: Produce a tailored orientation brief that hands off a task to a fresh agent or engineer with the right context, no more. Surfaces the 3 to 5 most relevant ADRs, recent plans, area-specific conventions, verification commands, and any MCP policy that applies. Use when starting a new chat session on a familiar repo, when handing work to a teammate, when a fresh agent joins a long task, or when you want to "compact" a noisy session into a clean briefing. Triggers on "onboard a new agent to this", "brief me on this area", "what context does this task need", "/gw onboard", "handoff to fresh agent". ([IgniticAB/groundwork](https://github.com/IgniticAB/groundwork/blob/HEAD/skills/groundwork/commands/onboard.md) · ★1)
- **groundwork-plan**: Produce a Plan Mode contract before any non-trivial change. Names files touched, approach, verification commands, and rollback. Required for any change touching more than three files, any refactor, any public-API change, or any migration. Triggers on "plan this change", "draft a plan for...", "before I start coding...", "/gw plan", "/plan mode". ([IgniticAB/groundwork](https://github.com/IgniticAB/groundwork/blob/HEAD/skills/groundwork/commands/plan.md) · ★1)
- **groundwork-report**: Sweep for new subagent transcripts and report what the captured runs cost, by role. ([amitbaz/ai-marketplace](https://github.com/amitbaz/ai-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/groundwork/commands/groundwork-report.md))
- **groundwork-scope**: Generate file-pattern-scoped rules so the right context loads for the right files. Use this when the repo has multiple stacks (frontend + backend, app + infra), or when global rules keep firing in the wrong places. Triggers on "scope these rules", "per-directory rules", "frontend rules separate from backend", "/gw scope". ([IgniticAB/groundwork](https://github.com/IgniticAB/groundwork/blob/HEAD/skills/groundwork/commands/scope.md) · ★1)
- **groundwork-self-review**: Periodic groundwork self-review — surface candidate improvements, never auto-apply. ([groundwork-md/groundwork](https://github.com/groundwork-md/groundwork/blob/HEAD/claude/commands/groundwork-self-review.md))
- **groundwork-stats**: Show whether transcript capture is on for this project and what has been captured so far. ([amitbaz/ai-marketplace](https://github.com/amitbaz/ai-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/groundwork/commands/groundwork-stats.md))
- **groundwork-verify**: Set up verification loops so the agent can self-correct. Detects test/lint/typecheck/build commands, bakes them into the context files, and wires up a pre-commit hook that keeps context current. The single highest-leverage move in this skill. Triggers on "add verification", "wire up tests for the agent", "self-correcting loop", "/gw verify". ([IgniticAB/groundwork](https://github.com/IgniticAB/groundwork/blob/HEAD/skills/groundwork/commands/verify.md) · ★1)
